# Agents · S · part 21

943 entries, skin-designer to so-docs. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **skin-designer**: Aplica los 3 skins canónicos (classic, retro, neon) a un juego concreto de Arcade Vault indicado por el usuario. Trabaja un juego a la vez — no audita ni modifica otros. Implementa directamente sobre components/games/&lt;Juego&gt;.tsx siguiendo el patrón de TetrisGame, y registra el progreso en references/game-with-themes.md. Úsalo cuando el usuario diga "aplica skins a &lt;juego&gt;", "añade skin &lt;x&gt; a &lt;juego&gt;", "diseña los skins de &lt;juego&gt;" o similar. ([Klerith/arcade-vault](https://github.com/Klerith/arcade-vault/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skin-designer.md) · ★6 · 2 copies)
- **skin-designer**: Diseña las skins de los juegos de Arcade E1230. Todo juego con motor debe tener al menos tres skins —Clásico (por defecto), Neón y Retro— que luzcan bien en modo oscuro y sean responsive. En modo auditoría revisa qué juegos las cumplen; en modo aplicar recibe UN juego nombrado por el usuario y escribe specs/NN-&lt;id&gt;-skins.md en Borrador (el primero incluye la base común del sistema de skins). Lleva el registro en references/game-skins.md. No escribe código. ([e1230/arcade-e1230](https://github.com/e1230/arcade-e1230/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skin-designer.md) · ★1)
- **skin-designer**: Diseñador de skins de Arcade Vault. Recibe UN juego (id de lib/games.ts) y le implementa las 3 skins obligatorias — clasico (por defecto), neon y retro — cada una con variante oscura y clara, cuidando que el modo oscuro tenga buen contraste. Trabaja solo el juego que se le indica, verifica con Playwright y actualiza references/templates/game-width-themes.md. Úsalo cuando se pida "añade/revisa las skins de &lt;juego&gt;" o "¿qué juegos tienen skins?". ([alfaaereo/05-arcade-vault](https://github.com/alfaaereo/05-arcade-vault/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skin-designer.md))

Implementa directamente sobre public/games/&lt;id&gt;/game.js y app/_games/&lt;id&gt;/&lt;PascalId&gt;Canvas.tsx siguiendo el patrón de Tetris, y registra el progreso en references/game-with-themes.md. Audita el motor indicado contra el contrato de skins, define o reutiliza las paletas, escribe el código de lib/games/skins.ts y del motor, y registra el avance en references/game-with-themes.md. - **skin-designer**: Audita e implementa los 3 skins obligatorios de todo juego con engine real en Arcade Vault: clásico (default), neon y retro, definidos en components/games/skins.ts. Verifica contraste ≥ 4.5:1 sobre el fondo negro del CRT y migra literales hex/rgba sueltos del engine a tokens del skin. Mantiene memoria en references/skin-audit.md. Úsalo cuando agregues un juego nuevo o sospeches que un juego existente rompe alguno de los 3 skins. ([carlosed7641/arcade-vault](https://github.com/carlosed7641/arcade-vault/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skin-designer.md))
- **skin-designer**: Recibe un juego ya implementado de Arcade Vault (`app/juego/&lt;slug&gt;/jugar/`) y garantiza que ofrezca al menos 3 skins seleccionables — Neon, Retro y Clásico, siendo Clásico el skin por defecto. Primero VALIDA contra la convención del repo (paleta por engine + selector UI) y, si falta algo, lo IMPLEMENTA extrayendo los colores del `engine.ts` a paletas, cableando la opción de tema y añadiendo el selector en `page.tsx`, sin alterar la jugabilidad. Verifica visualmente con capturas. NO crea juegos nuevos, NO toca el catálogo ni la base de datos. Úsalo cuando el usuario diga "revisa los skins de &lt;… ([dpperalta/arcade-vault](https://github.com/dpperalta/arcade-vault/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skin-designer.md))
- **skin-designer**: Adds the three canvas skins (Clásico, Neón, Retro) to one already-implemented Arcade Vault game. Refactors that game's hardcoded draw colors into a per-game palette module, wires the platform skin seam the first time it is needed, and records the palettes in .claude/skin-designer/palettes.md. Refuses and writes nothing if the named game has no engine. ([JamesPagani/arcade-vault-claude](https://github.com/JamesPagani/arcade-vault-claude/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skin-designer.md))
- **skin-designer**: Añade tres skins (`classic`, `neon`, `retro`) a un juego concreto de Arcade Vault y un selector dentro del propio juego para alternarlas. Se invoca con un `&lt;game-id&gt;` del `lib/game-registry.ts` como argumento obligatorio y toca solo ese juego. La primera vez que se usa introduce la infraestructura compartida (tipo `SkinId`, parámetro `skin` en `CreateEngine`, componente `GameSkinPicker` dentro del player); en invocaciones siguientes la reutiliza. Mantiene el seguimiento en `references/games-with-themes.md`. No crea specs, no toca Supabase ni `localStorage`, no itera varios juegos a la vez, no… ([jldoradomoles/arcade-vault](https://github.com/jldoradomoles/arcade-vault/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skin-designer.md))

Skinner is a rigorous, read-only senior code reviewer that checks correctness, security, error handling, conventions, architecture, and testability against the ticket's acceptance criteria and the project's CLAUDE.md. - **skip-incoming-review-only-for-trusted-input**: Incoming task messages default to requiring user approval before the agent runs. From 28.1, `Agent Task Message Builder.SetRequiresReview(false)` starts the agent immediately. That is safe only for inputs you already validated in AL (your page action, your posting subscriber). External email or partner payloads are not trusted by default. Analysis Warnings still force a review. Използвай преди деплой, при бавен сайт, регресия на Core Web Vitals (LCP/TBT/CLS) или нужда от сравнение преди/след; и когато някой каже „пусни PageSpeed преди да качим“, „защо не е 100“ или „сайтът е бавен“. - **Skraft - Acceptance Designer**: Use when transforming refined stories and architecture decisions into executable BDD scenarios and implementation plans, before any code is written. Activate on "distill", "acceptance scenarios", "gherkin", "test plan", "prepare for implementation", or when the SDLC pipeline enters DISTILL phase. ([SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin](https://github.com/SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/skraft-framework/com.github.copilot/agents/acceptance-designer.agent.md) · ★15)
- **Skraft - Acceptance Designer Reviewer**: Use when reviewing BDD scenarios, test plans, or implementation plans for completeness, business alignment, and testability gaps. Dispatched after acceptance-designer produces DISTILL artefacts, or manually to audit existing Gherkin scenarios. ([SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin](https://github.com/SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/skraft-framework/com.github.copilot/agents/acceptance-designer-reviewer.agent.md) · ★15)
- **Skraft - Backlog Discoverer**: Use when discovering, triaging, or prioritizing GitHub issues for a project. Supports three discovery modes: user-assigned issues, artifact-driven discovery from code changes, and search-based exploration. Activate on 'discover backlog', 'triage issues', 'what should I work on', 'find open issues', or at the start of engineering work before invoking the skraft orchestrator. Runs standalone (product layer) — the developer invokes it directly; it is not part of the skraft engineering pipeline. ([SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin](https://github.com/SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/skraft-backlog/com.github.copilot/agents/backlog-discoverer.agent.md) · ★15)
- **Skraft - Backlog Planner**: Use when refining raw GitHub issues into well-structured user stories with acceptance criteria, effort estimation, and milestone assignment. Activate on "refine", "plan sprint", "write stories", "split issue", "milestone planning", or when preparing a refined story before handing off to the skraft engineering orchestrator. Runs standalone (product layer) — the developer invokes it directly; it is not part of the skraft engineering pipeline. - **Skraft - Brownfield Analyst**: Use when the human chooses to analyze an existing/brownfield or legacy codebase that has no product docs or backlog — reverse-engineer it and produce a structured PRD (docs/prds/) that backlog tooling can turn into issues and user stories. Activate on 'analyze this codebase', 'bootstrap a PRD', 'reverse-engineer a PRD', 'document this legacy system', 'produce a PRD from existing code', 'no docs, start from the code'. Standalone workflow — the human invokes it directly; it is not a Skraft - Orchestrator phase. - **Skraft - Brownfield Harness Builder**: Use when the human wants to make an existing/brownfield service SAFE TO CHANGE before refactoring it — discover or reconstruct its API contracts, stand up Microcks mocks, and produce characterization (golden-master) tests that lock in CURRENT behavior, bugs included. Activate on 'build a safety net for this service', 'characterize this API before refactoring', 'lock in current behavior', 'set up contract tests for this legacy service'. - **Skraft - Brownfield Refactorer**: Use when the human wants to refactor or replace part of an existing brownfield codebase that already has a safety net (characterization/contract tests) — recommend Mikado (in-place restructuring) or Strangler Fig (incremental replacement behind a facade), let the human choose, then drive the work leaf-by-leaf or slice-by-slice, keeping the safety net green at every commit. Activate on 'refactor this module safely', 'apply Mikado to this change', 'strangle this component', 'replace this service incrementally'. Standalone workflow — the human invokes it directly; it is not a Skraft - Orchestrat… ([SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin](https://github.com/SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/skraft-framework/com.github.copilot/agents/brownfield-refactorer.agent.md) · ★15)
- **Skraft - Orchestrator**: Use when running the SKRAFT engineering pipeline from research to delivery (RESEARCH -&gt; DESIGN -&gt; DISTILL -&gt; DELIVER). Autonomous pipeline orchestrator that sequences phases, dispatches subagents, and persists resumable state. Consumes refined stories from the product layer; it does NOT do backlog discovery or story refinement (the separate skraft-backlog plugin, or any tool that writes the story files). Automatically resumes from the last persisted state. Handles all phase transitions, reviewer verdicts with retry logic, and the engineer-reviewer implementation loop. - **Skraft - Software Engineer**: [Internal subagent — dispatched by Skraft - Orchestrator only] Delivers code via Outside-In TDD and Clean Architecture. Full PREPARE → RED → SYNTHESIZE-GREEN → COMMIT cycle with Object Calisthenics, mutation testing gates, and strict test integrity. ([SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin](https://github.com/SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/skraft-framework/com.github.copilot/agents/software-engineer.agent.md) · ★15)

- **slop-stripper**: Behavior-preserving AI-slop strip pass. - **slop-verifier**: Fresh-context adversarial verifier. Given an artifact and an existing slop review or rewrite, it independently re-checks the claims, the citations, the package names, the scanner results, and the review's own discipline. It tries to break the review rather than confirm it. Reports which findings survive, which are unsupported, and which real defects the review missed. Never rewrites, never approves, never states or implies who or what wrote anything. Use before shipping a review or after a repair pass. - **snr-architect**: Principal engineer for system design and structural review. Use to design a new system, plan a significant change, or assess an existing architecture — boundaries, coupling, transaction and validation seams, failure model, one-way doors, and the first scaling bottleneck. Returns a plan or an assessment with ADRs; does not write implementation code. ([Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills](https://github.com/Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/snr-architect.md) · ★3)
- **snr-cloud-auditor**: Cloud, PaaS and container security reviewer. Use for a focused pass over how secrets, identities, networks, containers and infrastructure-as-code limit the damage if a hosting platform, CI or secrets store is breached — returns findings with blast radius, a secrets-ladder table, fixes and verification. Read-only; proposes changes rather than applying them. Defensive security only. ([Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills](https://github.com/Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/snr-cloud-auditor.md) · ★3)
- **snr-data-auditor**: Database and data-layer reviewer. Use for a focused pass over schema, constraints, indexes, query cost, N+1, migrations, transactions, multi-tenancy, backups and data retention/deletion — returning measured findings with plans, fixes and verification. Read-only; never runs a migration or a destructive statement. ([Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills](https://github.com/Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/snr-data-auditor.md) · ★3)
- **snr-incident-responder**: Security incident responder. Use when a provider or platform announces a breach, a credential leaks, a critical runtime CVE is being exploited, or a server may be compromised — returns a classified incident, an ordered rotation plan with verification, a hunt plan per log source and a notification flag. Read-only investigation; proposes actions rather than executing destructive ones. Defensive security only. ([Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills](https://github.com/Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/snr-incident-responder.md) · ★3)
- **snr-pentester**: Authorised penetration tester. Use for a scoped, non-destructive security test of a web app, API or cloud configuration the user owns or has written authorisation to test — builds a risk-ordered test plan, enforces the scope file before any traffic, proves findings with the least harmful evidence and reports each with a fix, a regression test and a detection signal. Refuses to act without a scope. Defensive, authorised testing only. ([Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills](https://github.com/Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/snr-pentester.md) · ★3)
- **snr-php-auditor**: PHP security reviewer. Use for a focused pass over a PHP codebase and its runtime — every PHP version and SAPI in play, status against the October 2026 php2shell fixes (CVE-2026-109618, CVE-2026-109619), php.ini and FPM configuration, PHP-specific code flaws (deserialization, includes, uploads, injection) and Composer, WordPress, Laravel and Symfony hygiene. Read-only; proposes fixes rather than applying them. Defensive security only. ([Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills](https://github.com/Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/snr-php-auditor.md) · ★3)
- **snr-security-auditor**: Application security reviewer. Use for a focused security pass over a codebase or a change — secrets, object-level authorization, injection, authentication, headers, supply chain — returning confirmed findings with locations, reproductions, fixes and regression tests. Read-only; proposes fixes rather than applying them. Defensive security only. ([Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills](https://github.com/Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/snr-security-auditor.md) · ★3)
- **snr-web-auditor**: Public web surface reviewer. Use for a focused pass over SEO, metadata, structured data, 404 and robots, internal linking, accessibility (WCAG 2.2 AA), cookie consent and the trust/conversion layer — returning a per-page findings table with suggested replacement copy. Read-only; a handful of GET requests, no crawling. ([Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills](https://github.com/Kin9Zeus/senior-engineer-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/snr-web-auditor.md) · ★3)
